Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The systems operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 723. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 481 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the systems decomposition. By minimality, the element a^723 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 481-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 213.4 (Invariance Principle)
